### [101] [Dimension comparison for Student's statistic under symmetric unimodality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26421)
*Jacopo Lenzi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了自标准化均匀变量和的尾部概率，在r=3时Student统计量的双边Edgeworth展开中首项敏感项消失，并分析了在移动边界rn=3+λ/n下的展开差异，得出一个显式的相位面Hδ(λ)。通过Khintchine尺度混合表示，找到了一个与n无关的光滑对称单峰分布，其Student尾部在足够大的n下超过同尺度的均匀分布尾部。同时证明均匀分布在偶数矩和非负系数偶次幂级数上达到最大，并推导出固定置信维度展开及在n=6时的精确反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自标准化和在临界点r=3附近的高阶渐近行为，特别是在不同删除策略下尾部概率的变化规律，并探索是否存在某个分布使其Student化尾部超过均匀分布。

Method: 利用Edgeworth展开分析Student统计量在移动边界r_n=3+λ/n下的渐近行为，结合Khintchine尺度混合表示构造特定父分布，并比较不同分布的尾部性质；使用分布矩不等式和相位面收敛性进行理论推导。

Result: 得到了在不同维度n和n−k下首项非零差的极限为显式相位面H_δ(λ)，其零曲线统一了多种删除方式；构造了一个C^∞紧支撑对称单峰分布，其Student尾部长期超过均匀分布；证明了均匀分布在所有偶数矩和非负系数偶次幂级数中最大化；并发现固定置信维度展开及在n=6时存在精确反转现象。

Conclusion: 尽管均匀分布在偶数矩意义下具有极大性，但其Student化尾部并非最大；存在其他光滑对称分布可在高维下产生更重的尾部；该现象由高阶渐近结构中的相位效应驱动，并在有限样本（如n=6）中出现反转，揭示了尾部行为的复杂性。

Abstract: Let $q_n(r)$ denote the tail probability at $r$ of the self-normalized sum of $n$ independent centered uniform variables. At $r=3$, the first distribution-sensitive term in the two-sided Edgeworth expansion of Student's statistic vanishes. We evaluate the expansion at the common moving boundary $r_n=3+λ/n$ in dimensions $n$ and $n-k$. Uniformly over deletion ranks retaining a fixed positive fraction of observations, the first nonzero difference converges to an explicit phase surface $H_δ(λ)$; its zero curve unifies fixed, sublinear and fixed-fraction deletions, with tangent crossing $12/35$. Through the Khintchine scale-mixture representation, this comparison yields a single compactly supported $C^\infty$ symmetric unimodal parent, independent of $n$, whose Student tail exceeds the equal-scale uniform tail for all sufficiently large $n$ along nominal levels tending to $2\{1-Φ(\sqrt3)\}$ from below. In contrast, a quantile-ratio order shows that the uniform parent maximizes every even moment and every convergent even power series with nonnegative coefficients. We also derive the fixed-confidence dimension expansion and an exact reversal at $n=6$.

</details>


### [102] [Posterior consistency for subdiffusion inverse problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26536)
*Haoyu Lu,Shaokang Zu,Junxiong Jia*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 研究了基于Whittle-Matérn高斯先验的半线性时间分数阶扩散方程初始状态的贝叶斯估计，证明了解的正则性、后验收缩率，并建立了预测误差和参数估计的极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在噪声观测的情况下，准确恢复时间分数阶非线性扩散方程的初始状态是一个具有挑战性的问题，本文旨在建立相应的贝叶斯推断理论基础。

Method: 采用基于Whittle--Matérn过程的重缩放高斯先验对未知初始条件建模，利用Lipschitz非线性条件证明解的H^{2+κ}正则性，并通过波包集构造和KL散度控制证明极小极大下界。

Result: 得到了L^2范数下的预测误差和Sobolev范数下的参数估计后验收缩率，其速率依赖于先验平滑性和空间维度；同时证明了相应的极小极大下界，验证了估计的最优性。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯方法能够有效恢复分数阶非线性扩散方程的初始状态，且在多项式速率下实现最优收敛。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian recovery of the initial state in a semilinear time-fractional subdiffusion equation from noisy random space-time point observations. A rescaled Gaussian prior based on a Whittle--Matérn process is assigned to the unknown initial condition. We prove the \(H^{2+κ}\)-regularity of the solution when the nonlinearity satisfies a Lipschitz condition in the \(H^κ\)-norm. We then establish posterior contraction rates for the prediction error in the \(L^2\)-norm and for the parameter in Sobolev norms. The rates are polynomial in the sample size, with exponent depending on the prior smoothness and the spatial dimension. Moreover, we prove a minimax lower bound by constructing a wavelet-packing set and controlling the Kullback--Leibler divergences.

</details>


### [103] [How far can symmetry help? Phase transitions and symmetry selection in sparse functional data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27055)
*Jocelyn Nembe*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏函数数据分析中协方差曲面的相变现象，探讨了域对称性（如循环群）如何影响采样阈值，并发现对称性可将阈值从经典值降低至n^{1/(4β)}，但存在饱和效应；数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在稀疏函数数据中，当数据具有对称性时，协方差估计的风险相变行为如何变化，尤其是对称性对采样阈值的影响。

Method: 利用傅里叶分析、泊松求和公式和核正定性，结合群作用下的样本对计数分析，推导出对称性影响下的风险上下界，并通过数值模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 发现循环群对称性将相变阈值从n^{1/(2β)}降至n^{1/(2β)}q^{-1/2}，且存在饱和效应；完全圆群对称性最多将其降至n^{1/(4β)}；近似对称性引入额外误差项并导致设计平面出现三个采样区域。

Conclusion: 对称性可显著降低稀疏函数数据分析中的采样需求，但其提升作用有限且存在理论下限；重参数化不影响阈值，而对称性通过减少有效方差起作用。

Abstract: In sparse functional data analysis, where $n$ curves are each observed at $m$ random points, the covariance surface undergoes a sharp phase transition: if the covariance has smoothness $β$, the risk drops from the two-dimensional nonparametric rate to the parametric rate $n^{-1}$ once $m$ exceeds $m^*_n \asymp n^{1/(2β)}$. We determine what a symmetry of the domain does to that transition. A cyclic group of order $q$ preserving process and design displaces the threshold to $n^{1/(2β)}q^{-1/2}$; the exponent is a square root because symmetry acts on the number of usable pairs, which enters the variance quadratically, while the parametric floor is untouched by group averaging. The displacement saturates: once the orbit is finer than the bandwidth the reduction factor is $\min(q,c_K/h)$, by Poisson summation and positive definiteness of the kernel autocorrelation, and beyond that point the rate collapses to the one-dimensional nonparametric rate. Hence no rotation symmetry, even the full circle group, lowers the threshold below $n^{1/(4β)}$; this floor is attained from above by our estimator and from below, up to a polynomial factor, by our lower bounds. Symmetry thus takes one halfway on a logarithmic scale from the classical threshold to constant sampling. The uniform lower bound rests on a positivity-preserving packing of the stationary sub-class; closing the gap is left open. If the symmetry is only approximate, the risk acquires an approximation term and the design plane splits into three regimes, one unreachable by additional sampling; the symmetry spectrum governing it is explicit in Fourier coordinates, and a hold-out procedure selects the symmetry level with leading constant one below saturation and within an absolute constant beyond. All laws above are confirmed numerically. Reparametrisation of the domain, by contrast, leaves the threshold unchanged.

</details>


### [104] [Connecting Riemannian Geometry and Statistical Inference for Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27209)
*Argyn Kuketayev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将2019年提出的商仿射度量与1970年Jennrich的相关矩阵等价性检验联系起来，揭示了Jennrich统计量本质上是商仿射度量张量的一半，并证明其在零假设下渐近服从卡方分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于全秩相关矩阵的商仿射度量缺乏闭式解和已知的渐近零分布，作者旨在建立该几何结构与经典统计检验之间的联系，以提供理论支持和解释。

Method: 通过分析高斯Fisher信息中消除边缘标准差的过程，发现其等价于对角缩放的商空间投影，从而将Jennrich统计量与商仿射度量张量关联起来，并推导其渐近分布。

Result: Jennrich统计量恰好等于商仿射度量张量的一半；在共同总体相关矩阵的两个独立高斯样本下，尺度化的平方测地距离收敛到4倍自由度为d=p(p-1)/2的卡方分布；当p=2时退化为两样本Fisher z检验。

Conclusion: Jennrich检验具有明确的黎曼几何解释，其统计量直接对应商仿射几何中的局部二次型，为相关矩阵比较提供了内在几何基础和渐近理论支持。

Abstract: The quotient-affine metric gives an intrinsic Riemannian geometry to full-rank correlation matrices, but its geodesic distance has no closed form and we are not aware of an analytic asymptotic null distribution for it. We connect this geometry, introduced in 2019, with Jennrich's 1970 asymptotic test for equality of correlation matrices. The quadratic form underlying Jennrich's statistic is exactly one half of the quotient-affine metric tensor. The identity arises because eliminating marginal standard deviations from Gaussian Fisher information performs the same projection as quotienting out diagonal rescalings. Jennrich's statistic therefore evaluates the local quotient-affine quadratic form directly. Moreover, for two independent Gaussian samples with a common population correlation matrix, the squared geodesic distance, scaled by effective sample size, converges in distribution to $4χ^2_d$, where $d = p(p-1)/2$. For $p=2$, the result reduces to the two-sample Fisher $z$ test.

</details>


### [105] [Conformal Prediction Through the Lens of Hypothesis Testing: Universality, Impossibility, and Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27310)
*Ryan J. Tibshirani,Rina Foygel Barber,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的视角来理解共形预测，即将其视为对样本交换性假设的置换检验的反转。这种方法不仅更符合传统的假设检验形式化，还能够利用经典假设检验理论重现共形预测中的基本普遍性和不可能性结果，并通过Neyman-Pearson理论推导出共形预测的最优性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形预测方法通常通过计算置换p值并反转以形成预测集，但本文试图从交换性假设的角度重新审视共形预测，以便更好地与传统假设检验理论对接，并揭示其理论基础和优化潜力。

Method: 作者将共形预测重新表述为对n+1个样本联合分布交换性的置换检验的反转，利用假设检验与置信集之间的对偶关系，结合Neyman、Lehmann、Scheffé等人的经典理论，分析共形预测的普遍性、不可能性及最优性。

Result: 研究发现，共形预测的基本理论结果（如普遍性和不可能性）可直接由经典假设检验理论推导得出；同时，基于Neyman-Pearson理论证明，在任意协变量与响应变量联合分布下，最优的预测集方法是一种使用Y|X条件密度倒数作为评分函数的共形预测器。

Conclusion: 将共形预测视为交换性假设检验的反转提供了一个更严谨的理论框架，不仅统一了已有结果，还揭示了其在有限样本下的最优性，强化了共形预测在统计学习中的理论地位。

Abstract: The connections between conformal prediction and permutation tests are already widely-known in the literature. Some authors motivate conformal prediction by saying that it computes a permutation p-value for the hypothesis $H_0 : Y_{n+1} = y$, and then inverts this to form a prediction set for $Y_{n+1}$ (i.e., accepts all values $y$ into the prediction set for which the p-value is large). In this paper, we examine an alternative view, which is less well-known: we again cast conformal prediction via the inversion of a permutation test, but for the null of exchangeability of the joint distribution of the $n+1$ samples. This change in perspective, while simple, adheres more closely to traditional formalization in hypothesis testing, which offers several benefits. First, we use the duality between conformal sets and testing to show that foundational universality and impossibility results in the conformal prediction literature can be reproduced directly using classical hypothesis testing theory (due to Neyman, Lehmann, Scheffé, Kraft, Le Cam, and others). Furthermore, we show that an optimality result for conformal prediction can be derived using standard Neyman-Pearson theory: for any joint distribution of the covariates and response $X,Y$, and any sample size, the optimal method for prediction sets---which delivers the most efficient set among all methods with valid coverage for exchangeable distributions---is a conformal predictor whose score is the inverse conditional density of $Y|X$.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Multi-Objective Planning for Healthcare Facility Resilience: Mitigate Now or Respond Later?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26304)
*Gizem Toplu-Tutay,John J. Hasenbein,Matt Kammer-Kerwick,Erhan Kutanoglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种双目标两阶段随机优化模型，用于在洪水风险下优化医疗设施的韧性投资与疏散决策，兼顾经济成本与社会脆弱性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对洪水对医疗设施造成的破坏和患者疏散问题，在不确定性条件下制定长期韧性投资策略，同时考虑经济成本与社会公平性。

Method: 构建了双目标两阶段随机优化模型，结合永久性设施加固与情景依赖的疏散决策；采用精确Benders分解和可扩展的拉格朗日对偶法，并设计自适应程序生成帕累托前沿。

Result: 在德克萨斯州3752个医疗机构的案例中验证模型，结果显示仅最小化经济损失会导致对社会脆弱地区保护不足，而适度调整可在增加少量成本的情况下显著降低服务中断影响；所提算法能有效求解大规模实例。

Conclusion: 综合考虑经济与社会影响的多目标优化方法可提升灾害风险管理中的公平性与效率，所提出的算法框架具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Flooding can damage healthcare facilities, interrupt local care capacity, and trigger costly patient evacuations. Addressing these risks requires long-term resilience investments under uncertainty. We develop a bi-objective two-stage stochastic optimization model that jointly determines permanent facility hardening and scenario-dependent evacuation decisions. The first objective minimizes expected evacuation, physical damage, and business-interruption costs. The second minimizes a service-disruption impact index that combines the duration and scale of service loss with a place-based social-vulnerability weight. We develop an exact Benders decomposition and a scalable Lagrangian-dual method with tailored primal recovery, embedding both within an adaptive procedure for constructing informative Pareto frontiers. A case study of 3,752 hospitals and nursing homes in Texas evaluates the framework under climate-informed tropical-cyclone flood scenarios. The results show that minimizing economic losses alone can systematically allocate less protection to facilities located in socially vulnerable areas, while moderate movement along the Pareto frontier can substantially reduce disruption impacts at limited additional expected cost. The exact decomposition solves all tested instances, including larger stress tests for which the extensive formulation becomes memory-limited, while the Lagrangian method provides substantially faster high-quality solutions.

</details>


### [107] [Directional Optimality Conditions for Optimization Problems in Asplund Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26328)
*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Asplund空间中约束优化问题的方向必要最优性条件，提出了方向度量次正则性的联合判据，并建立了其与方向伪正规性和拟正规性的关系，所得结果推广了有限维情形并得到新的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在Asplund空间中建立约束优化问题的更广泛适用的方向最优性条件，拓展已有有限维结果并揭示方向度量次正则性与其他方向性条件之间的关系。

Method: 利用极限次微分工具，在方向度量次正则性假设下推导方向最优性条件，并提出针对联合约束系统的方向度量次正则性联合判据，分析其与方向伪正规性、拟正规性的联系。

Result: 得到了基于极限次微分的方向最优性条件；提出了联合约束下的方向度量次正则性判据；建立了方向度量次正则性与方向伪正规性、拟正规性之间的关系；部分结果在有限维情形下也是全新的。

Conclusion: 本文成功将若干有限维方向性构造推广至Asplund空间，同时获得了即使在有限维中也新颖的最优性条件，丰富了变分分析与优化理论的框架。

Abstract: This paper develops directional necessary optimality conditions for constrained optimization problems in Asplund spaces. Under directional metric subregularity, we first derive a directional optimality condition in terms of limiting subdifferentials. We then introduce sufficient conditions for directional metric subregularity and establish their relationships with directional pseudo-normality and quasi-normality. For systems with joint constraints, we further propose a joint criterion for directional metric subregularity and use it to obtain necessary optimality conditions. The results not only extend several finite-dimensional directional constructions to an Asplund-space setting, but also yield conclusions that are new even in finite dimensions.

</details>


### [108] [Isoperimetric problems related to extremal diameter graphs in 3D: theoretical and numerical aspects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26395)
*Beniamin Bogosel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了三维空间中单位直径极值构型的对偶边长函数优化问题，通过几何分析和数值计算发现正四面体在极值构型中具有核心地位，并提出了与Blaschke-Lebesgue面积问题相关的结构猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解三维空间中固定直径图的极值构型结构，特别是分离函数在对偶边上的优化行为及其与经典几何问题（如Blaschke-Lebesgue问题）的联系。

Method: 结合几何证明（如球面Crofton公式）与数值方法（基于梯度的局部优化），对最多16个顶点的10,644个极值构型进行系统分析，重构对偶边对并处理顶点重合情况。

Result: 证明了极值构型序列在合并重合点后保留极值几何核心；给出了凹函数的锐利下界（由正四面体实现）；发现所有计算终点均包含正四面体；提出了关于四面体包含性与Blaschke-Lebesgue问题关联的猜想。

Conclusion: 正四面体在三维单位直径极值构型中扮演关键角色，研究结果为Blaschke-Lebesgue面积问题提供了新的视角和结构性猜想，尽管尚未认证全局最优性。

Abstract: We study optimization problems for separable functionals of the Euclidean or spherical lengths of dual edge pairs in finite extremal unit-diameter configurations in three dimensions. For a fixed diameter graph, these problems lead to nonconvex constrained optimization of the vertex coordinates. We first prove that a convergent sequence of extremal configurations retains an extremal geometric core after coincident points are merged and vertices incident to at most one diameter are removed. A spherical Crofton argument then gives a sharp lower bound for additive concave functionals, attained by the regular tetrahedron. We also analyze the effect of inserting or deleting dangling vertices. For the sum of products of spherical dual-edge lengths, we obtain an exact supremal reformulation of the three-dimensional Blaschke--Lebesgue area problem. The numerical study uses all 10,644 available extremal configurations with at most 16 vertices. We combine direct evaluation with gradient-based local optimization on each fixed graph and reconstruct the intrinsic diameter graph and its dual pairs after vertex collisions. For every supplied graph, the selected verified endpoint contains a regular tetrahedron. These computations do not certify the global maximum for any fixed graph, but they motivate structural conjectures connecting tetrahedral containment with the Blaschke--Lebesgue problem.

</details>


### [109] [Integrated Multi-Modal Transit Network Design via Dynamic Line Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26444)
*Ning Duan,Oktay Günlük,Samitha Samaranayake*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流的混合整数规划模型，联合优化公交线路规划和服务频率，并通过按需服务显式考虑首末公里连接，在固定运营预算下显著提升乘客量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决固定线路公共交通与按需出行服务集成中的建模挑战和大规模网络设计的计算难题。

Method: 采用基于流的混合整数规划建模方法，结合定制化的列生成启发式算法及定价子问题，实现城市尺度上的可计算性。

Result: 在波士顿和芝加哥的应用显示，相比仅公交、仅按需服务或多模式基准，乘客量分别最高提升20.99%、93.58%（波士顿）和10.63%、149.84%（芝加哥），优于基准方法5.42%-5.80%。

Conclusion: 联合协同设计公交线路、发车频率和按需接驳服务可在相同预算下显著提高 ridership，所提模型与求解方法能高效生成可行且高性能的运营方案。

Abstract: The integration of fixed-route public transit and on-demand mobility services presents both a modeling challenge and a computational opportunity for large-scale network design. We propose a flow-based mixed-integer programming formulation that jointly optimizes transit line planning and service frequencies while explicitly capturing first- and last-mile connectivity via on-demand services, under a fixed operating budget. To achieve tractability at urban scale, we develop a novel column generation heuristic scheme with tailored pricing subproblems. Applied to networks and demand in Boston and Chicago, the framework yields operationally feasible designs that substantially increase demand served. Relative to transit-only and on-demand-only baselines, ridership increases by up to 20.99% and 93.58% in Boston, and by up to 10.63% and 149.84% in Chicago. Compared to a multi-modal benchmark, our approach improves ridership by 5.42% and 5.80% in Boston and Chicago, respectively. These results demonstrate that (i) joint co-design of transit routes, frequencies, and on-demand legs within a unified optimization framework yields substantially greater ridership than single-mode or decoupled approaches under equivalent budget constraints; and (ii) the proposed formulation and column generation pricing scheme admit tractable, operationally feasible, high-performing solutions relative to tested baselines.

</details>


### [110] [Stability Analysis and Data-driven Verification via Recurrent Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26447)
*Roy Siegelmann,Fernando Paganini,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于递归李雅普诺夫函数（RLF）的稳定性分析新方法，通过将前向不变性放松为递归性，简化了传统李雅普诺夫函数的设计难点，并发展了仅依赖轨迹数据的GPU算法来验证实际稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统李雅普诺夫方法要求子水平集具有前向不变性，这在构造李雅普诺夫函数时带来困难，需要较强的技巧或计算成本；为此，作者希望放松这一强条件以降低构造难度。

Method: 引入递归性替代前向不变性，定义递归李雅普诺夫函数（RLF），并建立其与稳定性的关系；进一步提出基于范数的逆定理，并设计基于GPU的、仅使用轨迹数据即可验证稳定性的算法。

Result: 证明了在较弱条件下RLF可保证系统稳定，并可推广至渐近和指数稳定；提出了实用版本的 converse 定理，并实现了高效的数据驱动稳定性验证算法，复杂度仅为O(log(1/ε))。

Conclusion: RLF框架放宽了经典李雅普诺夫方法的限制，为稳定性分析提供了更灵活的工具，同时支持高效的数据驱动实现，揭示了性能与代价之间的内在权衡。

Abstract: Lyapunov's direct method is a cornerstone of stability and control, but it hinges on finding a Lyapunov function, a task demanding ingenuity or computation. A key difficulty is that every sub-level set of the function must be forward invariant, coupling its geometry to the system's trajectories. We relax this by replacing invariance with recurrence: a set is recurrent if every trajectory starting in it returns within a finite time. This yields the notion of a Recurrent Lyapunov Function (RLF), whose sub-level sets need only be recurrent. We show that, under mild conditions, RLFs guarantee stability, and we introduce stronger notions yielding asymptotic and exponential stability. We also give norm-based converse theorems: under the corresponding stability conditions, any norm is an RLF for their practical versions. We then develop GPU-based algorithms that certify (practical) stability from trajectory data alone, without a Lyapunov function. Certifying stability up to an $\varepsilon$-neighborhood needs only $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ trajectory evaluations, with constants growing as the certified decay rate nears the true one, exposing an intrinsic performance-cost trade-off.

</details>


### [111] [Galerkin approximations to the space of convex bodies by polytopes in nondegenerate V-representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26615)
*Janosch Rieger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于顶点表示的多面体对凸体空间进行有限维逼近的方法，研究参数锥的几何性质和不等式冗余，并构建收敛的近似序列以解决凸体空间中的全局优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在凸体空间中实现有效的数值计算和优化，需要对无限维的凸体空间进行有限维逼近。

Method: 利用给定方向集下的顶点表示多面体，构造一个由线性不等式定义的多面体凸子锥，并分析其几何结构与投影性质，建立嵌套Galerkin序列和定量逼近估计。

Result: 得到了关于单位球面覆盖密度的定量逼近误差估计，证明了近似误差局部一致收敛于零，并建立了约束全局优化问题有限维逼近的收敛性。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限维逼近方法能有效逼近凸体空间，并保证优化问题解的收敛性，为凸体上的数值优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce finite-dimensional approximations to the space of convex bodies based on polytopes in vertex representation. For a prescribed set of directions, the admissible point configurations form a polyhedral convex subcone of the Euclidean vector space, described by a finite system of linear inequalities. The interior of this cone contains only nondegenerate representations, in which all parameter points are distinct vertices of the represented polytope. We study the geometry of the parameter cone, redundancies in its defining inequalities, and natural projections of convex bodies onto the resulting polytope spaces. We derive quantitative approximation estimates in terms of how densely the prescribed directions cover the unit sphere and construct nested Galerkin sequences whose approximation error converges locally uniformly to zero. Finally, we use these approximation properties to establish the convergence of finite-dimensional approximations of constrained global optimization problems in the space of convex bodies.

</details>


### [112] [Quadratic Convexification of a Square Truncated by a Hyperbola](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26639)
*Yipeng Zhang,Yuyuan Ouyang,Boshi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致非凸集G的二次凸化，通过分类所有在G上非负的二次多项式的极端射线，并利用对偶性，得出了G提升凸包的精确有限半定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解具有曲边界的非凸集的几何特性，研究其在完全二次空间中的提升凸包。

Method: 通过分类在G上非负的二次多项式的所有极端射线，结合参数化的切线和双切线族，使用锥对偶方法进行分析。

Result: 得到了G的提升凸包的精确有限半定表示，揭示了有界几何带来的新的边界接触模式。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地捕捉到了非凸集G的复杂几何结构，并提供了其在二次空间中凸化的精确描述。

Abstract: We study the quadratic convexification of the compact nonconvex set ${G} := \left[1/2,2\right]^2 \cap \{(x_1,x_2)\in\mathbb R^2\mid x_1x_2\leq1\}, $ which is a square truncated by a hyperbolic arc. Although the ordinary convex hull of ${G}$ is a triangle, its lifted convex hull in the complete quadratic space retains the nontrivial geometry of the curved boundary. We characterize all extreme rays of the cone of quadratic polynomials nonnegative on $G$, including parameterized families of bounded tangent and bitangent rays. Using this classification and conic duality, we derive an exact finite semidefinite representation of the lifted convex hull of ${G}$. The analysis follows the general framework of our earlier work on an unbounded product-constrained region, but the bounded geometry creates new boundary-contact patterns and leads to a different finite organization of the nonnegative-quadratic cone.

</details>


### [113] [A Barrier Primal Dual Hybrid Gradient Method for Solving Linear Programming Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26667)
*Yingxin Zhou,Stefano Cipolla,Phan T. Vuong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的Barrier PDHG (BPDHG)算法，通过在PDHG框架中引入对数障碍函数来缓解线性规划问题中收敛缓慢的活跃集识别问题，并进一步发展为BPDLP方法，数值实验表明该方法能有效减少KKT残差的长期停滞现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDHG方法在求解过程中存在明显的两阶段收敛行为，其中长时间的活跃集识别阶段可能导致收敛速度变慢，本文旨在通过引入障碍函数来加速这一过程。

Method: 提出BPDHG算法，将对数障碍函数嵌入PDHG框架中，建立内层迭代的收敛性并推导误差界，证明外层序列收敛至最优解集；进一步将其集成到PDLP框架中形成BPDLP方法。

Result: 理论分析证明了BPDHG的收敛性，数值实验显示BPDLP在部分实例上能够有效缓解KKT残差的长期平台期现象，并提出了一个实例依赖的经验指标用于预测BPDLP相对于PDLP的优势场景。

Conclusion: 引入障碍函数的BPDHG/BPDLP方法可有效改善传统PDHG/PDLP在求解线性规划问题时的收敛行为，尤其在缓解收敛停滞方面表现良好，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Primal Dual Hybrid Gradient (PDHG) method has been verified to exhibit a two stage convergence behavior, in which a prolonged active set identification phase may be a major issue of slow convergence. In this paper, we propose Barrier PDHG (BPDHG), a nested algorithm which incorporates a logarithmic barrier function into the PDHG framework to alleviate this problem. We first establish convergence of the inner iterations, derive an error bound for the corresponding inner problem. Then we prove that the outer sequence generated by BPDHG approaches the optimal solution set of the LP problem we considered. Furthermore, we integrate the barrier technique into the {Primal Dual Linear Programming} (PDLP) framework to develop the corresponding Barrier PDLP (BPDLP) method. Numerical experiments show that the barrier modification can alleviate prolonged plateaus in the KKT residual on selected instances. We also investigate an empirical instance-dependent indicator for identifying LP problems on which BPDLP is more likely to outperform PDLP.

</details>


### [114] [Optimal Parameter-Free Gradient Minimization in $\ell_p$ Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26688)
*Shuting Yang,Yuning Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了在ℓ_p几何中通过一阶 oracle 找到梯度较小的查询点的复杂度，解决了Diakonikolas关于一般ℓ_p空间无参数优化的开放问题，在无需知道光滑常数L、初始距离R或最优值f*的情况下，实现了对不同p值的最优查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在未知光滑性和平稳尺度下，如何高效地在一阶oracle模型中找到具有小梯度的点的问题，特别是针对ℓ_p几何中的无参数自适应优化方法的设计与分析。

Method: 采用非退化的割线初始化策略，在不依赖已知L、R和f*的前提下，设计了一种适用于1<p<∞的参数自由优化方法，并结合局部尺度观测机制实现对不同p值的复杂度控制。

Result: 对于固定有限的p>2，建立了维度无关的确定性已知参数上界指数p/(p+2)，并在无需知晓L或R的情况下保持该指数；给出了不同p范围下的查询复杂度：O_p(\Kbar^{1/2})（1<p<2）、O(\Kbar^{1/2})（p=2）和O_p(\Kbar^{p/(p+2)})（p>2），并包含附加校准成本O_p(log(e+L/M_0))。

Conclusion: 成功解决了Diakonikolas提出的ℓ_p空间中无参数优化的开放问题，证明了在适当初始化条件下，即使缺乏先验信息，仍可实现近最优的查询复杂度，为自适应优化算法提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study the first-order oracle complexity of finding a queried point with small gradient in $\ell_p$ geometry, with particular attention to the information needed to adapt the unknown smoothness and distance scales. In the strict counted local value--gradient model, no finite complexity bound can depend only on $LR/\eps$ without a nondegenerate local scale observation: a one-dimensional construction keeps $LR/\eps=4$ while defeating every prescribed finite query budget.
  We resolve Diakonikolas's general-$\ell_p$ parameter-free extension question for every fixed $1<p<\infty$. Under a nondegenerate secant initialization, the method knows neither the smoothness constant $L$, the initial solution distance $R$, nor $f^*$, and returns a queried point $\widehat x$ with $\|\nabla f(\widehat x)\|_q\le\eps$. For fixed finite $p>2$, we first establish the dimension-free deterministic known-parameter upper exponent $p/(p+2)$ in $K=LR/\eps$, matching the published lower polynomial exponent under its horizon and dimension qualifications. The finite local routine fits the same observable scale--radius procedure, so this exponent is preserved without knowing $L$ or $R$. Writing $\Kbar=\max\{1,LR/\eps\}$, the post-initialization pair-oracle complexity is $O_p(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $1<p<2$, $O(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $p=2$, and $O_p(\Kbar^{p/(p+2)})$ for $p>2$, together with the additive calibration cost $O_p(\log(e+L/M_0))$ in every regime.

</details>


### [115] [When a Relaxed PEP Is Exact: The Sharp Queried-Gradient Rate of Nesterov's Fast Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26719)
*Yi Du*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了Nesterov快速梯度法在光滑凸函数上每一步查询梯度范数的最坏情况值，并构造了一个解析匹配族，填补了此前仅有的数值结果空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究虽给出了松弛PEP上界和部分数值精确解，但缺乏在整个时间范围上一致有效的解析匹配解族，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 利用FGM特定的球面多面体KN和标准投影包络函数构造函数族，通过一维球锥提升方法生成多面体顶点，并证明该构造达到已知上界。

Result: 对每个N≥7和维度d≥N−4，证明了最小查询梯度范数平方的上确界等于LR²除以∑_{k=0}^N t_k²，并给出了达到该界的显式构造。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Nesterov快速梯度法在任意步数下最小梯度范数的精确最坏情况界，并提供了统一的解析构造方案。

Abstract: We determine the exact worst-case value, at every horizon $N\geq7$, of the smallest queried gradient norm generated by Nesterov's fast gradient method on smooth convex functions. Let $t_0=1$ and $t_{k+1}=(1+\sqrt{1+4t_k^2})/2$, and let $x_0,\ldots,x_N$ denote the points at which the method evaluates gradients. For every such $N$ and every dimension $d\geq N-4$, we prove \[
  \sup_{\substack{f\in\F_{0,L}(\R^d),\ x_\star\in\arg\min f
  \norm{x_0-x_\star}\leq R}}
  \min_{0\leq k\leq N}\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^2
  =\frac{L^2R^2}{\sum_{k=0}^N t_k^2}. \] The relaxed-PEP upper bound is due to Kim and Fessler, who also reported tight numerical solutions of the exact-interpolation PEP at selected horizons. What remained missing was an analytic matching family valid uniformly over the horizon. For every $N\geq7$, we construct such a family using an FGM-specific spherical polytope $K_N$ and the standard projection-envelope function \[
  f_N(x)=\max_{g\in K_N}\left\{\ip{x}{g}-\frac12\norm{g}^2\right\},
  \qquad \nabla f_N(x)=\Proj_{K_N}(x). \] Every queried gradient has the same norm, and the vertices of $K_N$ are generated from a three-dimensional seed by a one-dimensional spherical cone lift. The lift preserves all projection inequalities and raises the adversary dimension by one at each horizon. The projection/Moreau-envelope template itself is classical; the new ingredients are the FGM-specific algebraic seed, the proof that it attains the relaxed bound, and the common-latitude lift that propagates this exactness to every $N\geq7$. We state precise hypotheses for that propagation and do not claim that every rank-one relaxed PEP admits such a seed.

</details>


### [116] [Lower Bounds for Nonconvex-PŁ Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26799)
*Siyu Pan,Jiajin Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在光滑非凸-波利亚克-洛贾斯维茨（NC-PŁ）极小极大优化中，使用确定性一阶 oracle 找到值函数驻点的复杂度。结果证明，在最坏情况下，达到特定精度所需的 oracle 查询次数存在下界，且该下界与已知上界在关键参数上的依赖关系一致，表明对条件数 κ 的线性依赖是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解在满足PŁ条件的非凸极小极大优化问题中，确定性一阶方法在寻找驻点时的理论极限，特别是条件数κ的影响是否可避免。

Method: 通过构造困难实例并分析任意确定性一阶方法在最坏情况下的性能，推导出达到ε-驻点所需的一阶oracle查询次数的下界。

Result: 证明了在给定平滑性和PŁ条件下，任何确定性一阶方法在最坏情况下都需要Ω(ℓΔκ/ε²)次oracle查询才能找到满足‖∇Φ(x)‖≤ε的解。

Conclusion: 该下界与已有上界匹配，说明对于确定性一阶方法而言，复杂度对条件数κ的线性依赖是固有且无法避免的。

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding stationary points of the value function in smooth nonconvex-Polyak-Łojasiewicz (NC-PŁ) minimax optimization. We assume that the objective is jointly $\ell$-smooth and satisfies the $μ$-PŁ condition in the dual variable, and that its value function $Φ(x):=\max_y f(x;y)$ satisfies $Φ(0)-\inf_xΦ(x)\leqΔ$. When $κ:=\ell/μ\gtrsim 1$ and $0<ε^2\lesssim\ellΔ$, we prove that every deterministic first-order method requires $Ω(\ellΔκ/ε^2)$ oracle queries in the worst case to find $x$ satisfying $\|\nablaΦ(x)\|\leqε$. This rate matches the known upper bound in its dependence on $(\ell,Δ,κ,ε)$ [Yang et al., 2022] and shows that the linear dependence on $κ$ is unavoidable for deterministic first-order methods.

</details>


### [117] [Mean-covariance turnpikes in Wasserstein distributionally robust linear-quadratic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26986)
*Yanzhi Wu,Zhengping Ji*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了基于Wasserstein惩罚的长时域随机线性系统的极小极大控制，提出了一种混合策略，实现了均值、协方差等变量在长时域下的指数趋稳，并证明了控制成本误差随终端层长度指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有经验扰动数据的离散时间随机线性系统，传统转轨分析方法难以适用，需发展新的理论工具处理时变均值与协方差动态下的长时域鲁棒控制问题。

Method: 通过建立降维的凸-凹Hamilton鞍点问题刻画非零均值参考轨迹，利用Wasserstein距离对扰动分布进行建模，并设计结合静态仿射反馈与有限时域终端调节的混合控制策略。

Result: 证明了均值状态、协态、控制输入、最坏扰动均值及闭环协方差在长时域下满足双边指数转轨估计；构造的混合策略使最坏情况下的成本差距随终端层长度指数衰减，且该性质对时域长度具有一致性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了基于经验数据的长时域鲁棒控制中的转轨行为分析难题，为降低计算开销提供了理论支持和实用策略。

Abstract: We study long-horizon Wasserstein-penalized minimax control for discrete-time stochastic linear systems with empirical disturbance data, in which adversarial disturbance distributions induce time-varying mean and covariance dynamics, making standard turnpike arguments not directly applicable. For possibly uncentered data, we characterize the generally nonzero mean reference through a reduced convex-concave Hamiltonian saddle problem. We prove horizon-uniform, two-sided exponential turnpike estimates for the mean state, adjoint, control, worst-case disturbance mean, and closed-loop covariance, showing that they spend the majority of time near static references when the horizon is long. We further construct a hybrid policy combining time-independent affine feedback with finite-horizon steering over a terminal layer, proving that its worst-case cost gap decays exponentially with the terminal-layer length uniformly in the horizon, which helps reducing the computation cost for long-horizon robust controls. Numerical examples illustrate the estimates and their dependence on the Wasserstein penalty.

</details>


### [118] [Discrete eigenvalue optimization from entropic smoothing and first-order methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27024)
*Deborah Hendrych,Mathieu Besançon,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于分支定界法、熵平滑和Frank-Wolfe方法的组合约束下最小特征值最大化新方法，适用于高维和结构化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在组合和整数约束下最大化最小特征值是一个具有挑战性的问题，传统方法在处理高维或复杂结构时效率较低。

Method: 结合分支定界法、最小特征值函数的熵平滑技术以及基于凹松弛的Frank-Wolfe方法，利用线性优化 oracle 处理组合结构，并引入基于秩和特征值的剪枝及对偶变量固定策略。

Result: 该方法在E最优实验设计和最大代数连通性问题上表现良好，尤其在高维和具有额外组合结构的问题上优于SCIP-SDP；而SCIP-SDP在约束较简单、规模适中的实例上表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在处理大规模和结构复杂的最小特征值最大化问题时具有显著优势，为组合半定规划提供了有效的求解途径。

Abstract: We study the maximization of the minimum eigenvalue under combinatorial and integrality constraints. We propose a new approach based on branch-and-bound combines entropic smoothing of the minimum eigenvalue function and Frank-Wolfe methods over concave relaxations of the constraints, thereby exploiting combinatorial structure through linear optimization oracles. We establish approximation and convergence guarantees, including for truncated gradients computed from partial eigendecompositions, and introduce rank- and eigenvalue-based pruning and duality-based variable fixing. We evaluate the method on E-optimal experimental design and maximum algebraic connectivity problems and compare it with SCIP-SDP. The results show that our approach is particularly effective for large-dimensional instances and problems with additional combinatorial structure, whereas SCIP-SDP performs better on moderately sized instances with simpler constraints.

</details>


### [119] [Exact low-dimensional reformulations for regularized spectral approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27052)
*Shengxiang Deng,Xudong Li,Yangjing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究了正则化谱逼近问题及其加权变体的精确低维重构，在无权重情况下利用弱主控单调性实现奇异值上的精确重构，而在加权情况下需满足兼容性和同时对角化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索正则化谱逼近问题在低维空间中的精确重构方法，特别是在加权情况下的适用条件。

Method: 利用弱主控单调性分析无权重情形，并在加权情形下引入兼容性和同时对角化条件进行理论推导，辅以反例验证条件必要性。

Result: 在无权重情况下实现了基于奇异值的精确低维重构；在满足特定条件下，加权情形也可精确重构；并通过反例证明若缺乏同时对角化，则重构可能失效。

Conclusion: 同时对角化是加权谱逼近问题实现精确低维重构的关键条件，缺失时无法保证重构有效性。

Abstract: We study exact low-dimensional reformulations for a regularized spectral approximation problem and its weighted variant. In the unweighted setting, weak-majorization monotonicity of the fidelity term enables an exact reformulation over singular values. In the weighted setting, we establish such a reformulation under compatibility and simultaneous diagonalization conditions. We further show, via a counterexample, that this exact reformulation can fail in the absence of simultaneous diagonalization.

</details>


### [120] [Classification of facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27062)
*Mitsuhiro Nishijima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了与欧几里得约旦代数相关的完全正锥的面暴露性，并根据其秩进行分类：秩不超过2时面暴露，秩大于等于5时不面暴露；秩为3或4时给出了具体刻画，并发现该分类与完全正锥的谱锥性分类一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解完全正锥在对称锥上的面暴露性及其与欧几里得约旦代数秩之间的关系，以建立其结构理论并揭示其几何特性。

Method: 通过分析相关欧几里得约旦代数的秩，结合面暴露性的定义和性质，对完全正锥的面暴露性进行分类，并与谱锥性结果进行比较。

Result: 证明了当秩≤2时完全正锥是面暴露的，当秩≥5时不是面暴露的；在秩为3或4的情况下给出了具体的面暴露性判据。

Conclusion: 完全正锥的面暴露性主要由对应欧几里得约旦代数的秩决定，且其分类结果与谱锥性的分类一致。

Abstract: We classify the facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones in terms of the rank of the associated Euclidean Jordan algebras. The completely positive cones are facially exposed when the rank is at most $2$, but are not facially exposed when the rank is at least $5$. Facial exposedness is not completely determined by the rank when the rank is $3$ or $4$, but we provide a characterization in each case. The resulting classification of facial exposedness in fact agrees with the corresponding classification of spectrahedrality for completely positive cones.

</details>


### [121] [Randomized Quasi-Gauss--Newton Methods for Solving General Nonlinear Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27084)
*Chengchang Liu,Luo Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Gram矩阵近似的随机拟高斯-牛顿法，用于求解一般非线性方程，在欠定和超定情形下分别实现了局部超线性收敛和与条件数无关的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟牛顿方法主要针对方阵系统，且要求初始雅可比估计接近真实值，限制了其适用性。本文旨在扩展至更一般的非线性方程求解场景。

Method: 通过逼近Gram矩阵构建随机拟高斯-牛顿方法，适用于欠定和超定非线性方程系统。

Result: 在欠定情况下实现对最优解的局部超线性收敛；在超定情况下实现对非线性最小二乘问题驻点的局部与条件数无关的收敛。

Conclusion: 所提方法拓宽了拟牛顿类算法的应用范围，降低了对初始条件的要求，增强了对不同类型非线性系统的适应能力。

Abstract: This paper considers the local convergence for solving general nonlinear equations. We establish randomized quasi-Gauss--Newton methods based on the approximation of the Gram matrix, addressing both the underdetermined and the overdetermined settings. For the underdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local superlinear convergence to the optimal solution. For the overdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local condition-number-free convergence to the stationary point of the nonlinear least-square formulation of the problem. In contrast, existing quasi-Newton methods mostly focus on square systems and additionally require the initial Jacobian estimate to be sufficiently close to the exact one.

</details>


### [122] [Co-Optimized Generation, Transmission, and Storage Expansion: System Value and Optimal Duration of Pumped-Storage Hydropower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27222)
*Rafael Benchimol Klausner,Rafael Kelman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种发电-输电-储能扩展规划框架，将储能时长作为优化结果的一部分，应用于巴西2030-2050年的电力系统。研究发现，允许多种持续时间的抽水蓄能（PSH）参与竞争可显著降低系统成本、运行成本和化石能源依赖，相比仅使用4小时电池储能（BESS）更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规划模型固定储能时长，无法充分挖掘不同储能时长对系统经济性和可靠性的潜在贡献。本文旨在通过将储能时长纳入优化过程，更准确地评估储能需求与系统价值。

Method: 构建了一个滚动时域、两阶段随机线性规划模型，每个五年阶段以全年每小时分辨率模拟，并考虑十种一致的来水、风能和太阳能场景；比较了包含多时长PSH与BESS的候选方案以及仅含BESS的方案。

Result: 当允许PSH参与时，模型选择建设31.2 GW / 755 GWh的PSH（以12小时为主，含部分72小时单元），不建设BESS；若无PSH，则建设38 GW / 152 GWh的BESS并额外增加7.8 GW燃气装机。PSH使2050年年化系统成本降低6.8%（50亿美元），运行成本降23.5%，火电发电量减少34 TWh/年，边际成本降20%，2035年VRE弃电率从8.4%降至3.2%。

Conclusion: 限制储能候选方案为单一时长会低估最优储能需求，高估对备用火电的需求；多时长储能竞争机制能更高效地满足长期储能和系统灵活性需求，该机制具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Expansion planning models usually fix storage duration before optimization, setting how much storage power to build but not for how long it can discharge. We present a generation-transmission-storage expansion framework in which candidates of many durations compete on annualized cost, making the duration mix an optimization outcome. It is a rolling-horizon, two-stage stochastic linear program, each five-year stage is a full year at hourly resolution under ten coherent inflow, wind, and solar scenarios. We apply it to the Brazilian Interconnected System over 2030-2050, where demand roughly doubles to 1,716 TWh/year and variable renewable energy (VRE) supplies most new capacity. Two cases are compared: 36 pumped-storage hydropower (PSH) candidates over four subsystems and nine durations (4-144 h) alongside 4-h battery energy storage systems (BESS), and BESS alone.
  With PSH available, the model builds 31.2 GW / 755 GWh of PSH and no BESS, dominated by 12-h capacity with 4.9 GW of 72-h units. Without PSH it builds 38 GW / 152 GWh of BESS and 7.8 GW more gas-fired capacity, mostly open-cycle peakers. PSH lowers 2050 annualized system cost by US$ 5.0 billion/year (6.8%), operating cost by 23.5%, thermal generation by 34 TWh/year, and long-run marginal cost by 20%; 2035 VRE curtailment falls from 8.4% to 3.2%. The magnitudes are specific to Brazil, but the underlying mechanism is general: limiting storage candidates to a single duration understates the system's optimal energy-storage requirement and overstates its residual need for thermal capacity.

</details>


### [123] [Asymptotic consensus and flocking under decaying persistent excitation on rooted digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27263)
*Chiara Cicolani,Elisa Continelli,Cristina Pignotti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有非通用交互、时间延迟和可能通信故障的一阶和二阶对齐模型，扩展了先前结果到仅假设为有根的交互有向图，并允许固定长度时间区间上的交互量随时间多项式衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已有模型以适应更弱的连通性和逐渐减弱的通信条件，研究在这些条件下群体行为是否仍能出现。

Method: 通过引入较弱的持续激励条件，分析一阶Hegselmann-Krause型模型和二阶Cucker-Smale模型在交互图仅为有根结构时的渐近行为。

Result: 对于一阶模型，在通信权重衰减指数与根节点最大距离之间满足适当条件时，证明了共识的渐近收敛；对于二阶模型，在影响函数衰减的额外假设下建立了渐近集群行为。

Conclusion: 即使在通信逐渐减弱且无需强连通性的情况下，集体行为仍然可以涌现。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate first- and second-order alignment models with non-universal interaction, time delays and possible communication failures, extending the results in [17] to interaction digraphs that are only assumed to be rooted and to a weaker Persistence Excitation Condition. In particular, we allow the amount of interaction over time intervals of fixed length to decay polynomially in time.
  For the first-order Hegselmann-Krause type model, we prove asymptotic convergence to consensus under a suitable condition relating the decay exponent of the communication weights to the maximal distance from the root. For the second-order Cucker-Smale model, we establish asymptotic flocking under an additional assumption on the decay of the influence function. These results show that collective behavior can still emerge under progressively weakening communication and without requiring strong connectivity of the interaction graph.

</details>


### [124] [Marine Autonomous Vehicle Fleet Scheduling to Maximise Scientific Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27271)
*Mehdi El Krari,Jonathan Smith,Maria Fox*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，用于自动化和优化海洋自主航行器（MAV）的部署调度，在满足操作约束的同时最大化数据采集并最小化能耗与使用数量，并通过可视化支持决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着海洋自主航行器（MAV） fleet 规模扩大，人工调度变得复杂且耗时，亟需自动化方法来提升大规模MAV作业的效率和可扩展性。

Method: 采用混合整数线性规划（MILP）建模，考虑电池容量、数据采集时间窗口等约束，以最大化数据采集、最小化车辆数量和能耗；创新性地整合了传统船只航线，支持中途电池更换和快速转场。

Result: 该模型具有高可扩展性，可在数秒内求解数十个MAV的路径问题，几分钟内扩展到数百个；同时具备‘what-if’场景模拟能力，并生成可视化结果以增强解释性和决策支持。

Conclusion: 所提出的MILP框架能高效、可扩展地优化大规模MAV部署，兼具实际调度功能与战略模拟能力，显著提升海洋观测任务的自动化与智能化水平。

Abstract: The marine science community increasingly relies on Marine Autonomous Vehicles (MAVs) to collect the critical environmental data required to understand global ocean systems. However, as these operations scale, manually routing and planning large autonomous fleets becomes exponentially complex and time-consuming. To address this, we propose a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model designed to automate and optimise MAV deployment schedules. The model accounts for strict operational constraints, including battery capacities and time windows for data collection, while aiming to maximise total data collection and minimise both the number of deployed vehicles and their energy consumption. A key novelty of this framework is integrating conventional ship itineraries, allowing MAVs to support vessels with mid-mission battery swapping or accelerated transit between waypoints. Computational experiments demonstrate that the model is highly scalable, solving routing problems for fleets of dozens of MAVs in seconds, and scaling to hundreds of vehicles in only a few minutes. Beyond operational scheduling, the framework serves as a robust simulation tool for evaluating 'what-if' scenarios and analysing the impact of varying parameters on deployment strategies. Finally, the solution generates a suite of visualisations designed to enhance explainability and support strategic decision-making for stakeholders.

</details>


### [125] [When MILP Beats QP: Piecewise-Linear Reformulations of Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27312)
*Quentin Ploussard,Maris Usis,Oluwabunmi Iwakin,Matija Pavičević*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的混合整数线性规划（MILP）方法来近似双线性项，并在一类称为顺序耦合双线性规划（SCBP）的问题上验证了其优越的计算性能，特别是在长序列场景下，使得开源MILP求解器能够超越专用QP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性项导致非凸二次规划问题难以全局求解，现有连续分段线性（CPWL）逼近方法受限于域划分方式，影响求解效率，因此需要更高效且结构紧凑的MILP建模方法。

Method: 利用单位双线性函数的对称性设计高精度CPWL逼近，理论推导其最大与平均逼近误差，并构建三种不同的MILP形式（Triangle、Square 和 DC），最后通过大量数值实验评估其在SCBP模型上的表现。

Result: 提出的“Square” MILP形式在SCBP实例中表现出最优的计算性能，尤其在长序列情况下显著优于直接QP求解器，且使开源MILP求解器具备竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过对称性利用和紧凑建模，所提出的“Square” MILP方法为求解含双线性项的优化问题提供了高效可行的替代路径，推动了非凸问题的可扩展求解。

Abstract: The presence of bilinear terms in mathematical modeling generally yields nonconvex quadratic programs (QPs) that remain computationally challenging to solve to global optimality. While continuous piecewise linear (CPWL) approximations can reformulate these nonlinearities into mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), the geometric construction of the domain partition heavily dictates the resulting solver efficiency. In this paper, we present efficient MILP formulations for approximating bilinear terms and rigorously evaluate their computational merits against direct QP solvers. First, we introduce CPWL approximations with arbitrary high degrees of accuracy that explicitly account for and exploit the inherent symmetries of the unit bilinear function. Second, we analytically establish the exact maximum and average approximation errors of the CPWL approximations. Third, we construct and compare three distinct MILP formulations (a ``Triangle'', a ``Square'', and a difference-of-convex ``DC'' formulation) of the CPWL functions. Fourth, we formalize a highly relevant class of optimization models, Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs (SCBP), where variables represent system states and state changes. Finally, through extensive numerical experiments, we demonstrate that our compact ``Square'' MILP formulation achieves superior computational performance on SCBP instances with long sequence horizons, allowing mature open-source MILP solvers to effectively outperform QP solvers.

</details>


### [126] [UGM: A Unified Framework and New Perspectives for Accelerated Gradient Methods in Smooth and Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27368)
*Danqing Zhou,Shiqian Ma,Junfeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于加速梯度方法的统一框架UGM，涵盖了多种针对L-光滑和μ-强凸函数优化设计的加速与常规梯度类方法。该框架的迭代更新可被解释为重球法与标准梯度下降的混合组合，揭示了经典加速方法在加速性与稳定性之间实现权衡的本质。通过李雅普诺夫函数进行统一收敛性分析，并据此设计了一类利用梯度与迭代变量内积关系来优化更新的新加速算法。在二次优化与逻辑回归实验中，新算法表现出优于现有基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一理解多种加速梯度方法并揭示其内在机制，需要构建一个涵盖广泛梯度类算法的通用框架，并通过理论分析指导更高效算法的设计。

Method: 提出名为UGM的统一框架，将迭代更新解释为重球法与梯度下降的混合动力系统；采用李雅普诺夫函数方法进行统一收敛性分析；基于梯度与迭代变量之间的内积关系设计增强型加速算法。

Result: 建立了涵盖多种经典方法的统一框架UGM；给出了加速方法在加速与稳定间权衡的物理解释；发展出一类新的加速算法；在二次优化和逻辑回归任务上验证了新算法的优越性能。

Conclusion: 加速梯度方法可通过统一的动力学视角进行建模与分析，引入保守梯度步可提升重球法的稳定性；基于内积信息优化更新能有效提升算法性能，为未来算法设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a unified framework for accelerated gradient methods, dubbed UGM, which subsumes a wide range of accelerated and conventional gradient-type methods designed for minimizing $L$-smooth and $μ$-strongly convex functions. We demonstrate that the iteration update of the proposed framework can be intrinsically interpreted as a hybrid combination of the heavy-ball method and vanilla gradient descent. This interpretation reveals that classical accelerated gradient methods essentially integrate a conservative gradient descent step into the fast yet unstable heavy-ball dynamics, which achieves a favorable trade-off between acceleration and stability. We further establish a unified convergence analysis using Lyapunov functions. Guided by our analysis, we develop a family of enhanced accelerated gradient algorithms that leverage the inner product relationship between gradient information and iterative variables to optimize iterative updates. Extensive numerical experiments on unconstrained quadratic optimization and logistic regression validate that the proposed algorithms achieve superior performance compared with existing baseline methods under typical structural conditions.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

### [127] [Spectral Fingerprints of Gauge Theories on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27457)
*Graham Van Goffrier,Debasish Banerjee,Bipasha Chakraborty,Emilie Huffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种聚焦于任意能谱区域的量子算法——最大混合态谱采样，用于研究从基态现象到中频谱热化行为的完整模型指纹，并展示了其在(1+1)d非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论中的应用及近期超导量子硬件上的实现潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了能够全面了解量子系统的能谱特性，包括基态和中频谱的行为，需要一种可以无偏地提取整个能量范围内的谱分布的方法。

Method: 采用最大混合态谱采样这一无偏量子算法，通过聚焦于所需的能谱区域来学习模型的全貌；利用Pauli-frame优化编译控制时间演化操作；结合新兴的动力学电路能力减少所需量子比特数，并简化量子读出误差缓解。

Result: 该方法能够在78个双量子比特门深度的电路中进行模拟，有效利用现有硬件资源；展示了对无序多体跃迁和中频谱态密度的应用案例及其后续处理步骤。

Conclusion: 最大混合态谱采样是一种有效的技术，可用于探索复杂量子系统的关键物理现象，在当前和近期的量子硬件上具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Maximally mixed state spectral sampling is an unbiased quantum algorithm that allows for extraction of a finite-resolution spectral distribution from a Hamiltonian over potentially the entire allowed range of energies. We show how it may be focused on any desired area of the spectrum in order to learn about the full \textit{fingerprint} of the model of interest: from its ground state phenomena such as quantum criticality, obtained from the lowest lying energies, to its thermalization behavior, obtained from the mid-spectrum. We demonstrate this technique specifically on a $(1+1)d$ non-Abelian $SO(3)$ gauge theory, providing a comprehensive analysis of the steps necessary for performing this algorithm, as well as what is possible in the near-term with superconducting quantum hardware, performing simulations with circuits that are $78$ two-qubit gates deep. We show how this algorithm is able to take advantage of emerging dynamical circuit capabilities in near-term hardware to roughly halve the number required qubits, as well as how quantum readout error mitigation is trivial for this method. Along the way, we propose a novel strategy for compiling the controlled-time evolutions needed for spectral sampling by means of Pauli-frame optimizations. We illustrate two physical applications of quantum spectral sampling -- disordered many-body transitions, and mid-spectrum densities of states -- and what postprocessing steps they require beyond the Fourier outputs of the algorithm.

</details>


### [128] [Least Variable Quantum Counting Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26240)
*Bita Olamaei,Florian Meier,Costantino Budroni,Pharnam Bakhshinezhad,Giuseppe Vitagliano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限内存资源下经典和量子计数过程的时间精度问题，提出了一个经典d态计数过程的有限内存方差界，并发现量子过程可以突破该界限，展现出优于经典过程的时序精度，揭示了量子计数在时间测量中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限内存条件下，如何精确计时是一个核心问题。作者旨在探究经典与量子计数过程在时间波动性上的根本差异，并寻找量子系统是否能在相同资源下实现更高时间精度。

Method: 通过建立一般性的有限维经典与量子计数过程框架，推导出经典d态过程的方差下限，并利用数值优化寻找违反该界限的量子过程；对单量子比特情况，在单Kraus无跳变族中解析推导了大均值下的边界。

Result: 1）得出并证明了经典d态计数过程的紧致方差下界，由离散Erlang型阶梯过程达到；2）通过数值优化发现某些量子过程能突破该经典界限，实现更低的首跳时间波动；3）在单量子比特情形下，解析证明在大均值极限下量子优势依然存在；4）最优量子过程表现出相干的条件动力学，并在均值增大时趋近于连续时间量子跃迁描述。

Conclusion: 量子计数过程可以在相同内存资源下超越经典过程的时间精度极限，展示了明确的有限内存量子优势，连接了离散计数过程与连续时间量子计时，为量子时间测量提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Counting processes provide a fundamental description of stochastic events ranging from photon detection to clock ticks. A central question is how accurately such events can be timed when only finite memory resources are available. Here, we investigate this problem within a general framework of finite-dimensional classical and quantum counting processes. We derive a rigorous finite-memory variance bound obeyed by every classical $d$-state counting process, which is tight and saturated by a discrete Erlang-type ladder process. Through numerical optimization, we identify quantum counting processes that violate this classical bound, achieving smaller first-tick fluctuations than any classical process with the same memory size and mean tick time. For the qubit case, we further derive an analytical large-mean bound within a single-Kraus no-tick family, showing that the quantum advantage persists asymptotically within this class. The optimized quantum processes exhibit coherent conditioned dynamics and approach a continuous-time quantum-jump description as the mean increases. Our results establish a finite-memory quantum advantage in temporal precision and connect discrete-time counting processes with continuous-time quantum timekeeping.

</details>


### [129] [Fast GHZ encoding with $1-o(1)$ fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26241)
*Laura Shou,T. C. Mooney,Twesh Upadhyaya,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用全对全二体哈密顿量在O(log N/N)时间内实现高保真度N量子比特GHZ态编码的协议，达到了理论下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高效、高保真度的多量子比特纠缠态制备，突破时间下限限制。

Method: 结合自旋压缩动力学与量子信号处理技术，利用有界二体相互作用的全对全2-局部哈密顿量。

Result: 实现了(1-o(1))保真度的GHZ态编码，时间为O(log N/N)，达到理论下限Ω(log N/N)，并适用于幂律相互作用哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该协议在时间和保真度上均达到最优，为快速生成多体纠缠态提供了理论和方法支持。

Abstract: We prove that $(1-o(1))$-fidelity $N$-qubit Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) encoding can be performed in time $O(\log N/N)$ using all-to-all 2-local Hamiltonians with bounded 2-qubit interaction terms. This saturates the theoretical lower bound $Ω(\log N/N)$, and by rescaling, also saturates the lower bound on signaling time for Hamiltonians with power-law interaction strengths $1/r^γ$, $γ<d$ in dimension $d$. The protocol uses spin-squeezing dynamics combined with quantum signal processing.

</details>


### [130] [Lieb-Schultz-Mattis Constraints for Quantum Channels: A Spacetime-Duality View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26266)
*Sarang Gopalakrishnan,Yu-Jie Liu,Tsung-Cheng Lu,Frank Pollmann,Yizhi You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放量子系统中李布-舒尔茨-马蒂斯（LSM）异常如何影响动力学行为和时间关联，通过时空对偶性将带有对称性异常的量子信道映射到高维对称保护拓扑相，并提出新的探针来诊断由LSM异常导致的长程时间序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子异常如何约束开放量子系统的动力学特征，特别是LSM定理在非平衡系统中的表现形式。

Method: 利用时空对偶性，将d维具有混合对称性异常的量子信道的李维尔算符映射到(d+1)维的混合态对称保护拓扑相；引入扭曲Renyi-N关联函数作为时间关联的探针，并分析李维尔奇异谱的对偶性质。

Result: 建立了开放量子信道与高维mSPT相之间的对应关系，发现初始态和稳态对应于边界态；TRNC关联函数对偶于mSPT的奇异关联函数，可用于探测长程时间序；李维尔奇异谱被揭示为比普通谱更基本的异常诊断工具，且对偶于mSPT的算符纠缠谱。

Conclusion: LSM异常通过时空对偶在开放量子系统中表现为高维拓扑相的边界特征，其动力学效应可通过TRNC和李维尔奇异谱有效探测，为理解非平衡系统中的量子异常提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum anomalies strongly constrain the possible behavior of many-body systems. A prime example is the Lieb-Schultz-Mattis (LSM) theorem, which relates UV symmetry and filling constraints to IR features of the energy spectrum and ground-state structure. Here, we ask how LSM constraints shape dynamical signatures and temporal correlations in open quantum systems. Using a spacetime duality, we show that the Liouvillian of a $d$-dimensional repeated quantum channel with a mixed anomaly between strong $S$ and weak $G$ symmetry can be mapped to a $(d{+}1)$-dimensional mixed-state symmetry-protected topological (mSPT) phase. Under this correspondence, the initial and steady states of the channel are identified with boundary states of the higher-dimensional mSPT in the presence of bulk projection. We further introduce the twisted Renyi-$N$ correlator (TRNC) as a probe of temporal correlations in the channel and demonstrate that it is dual to the mSPT strange correlator, providing a direct bulk-boundary route to diagnose long-range temporal order implied by the LSM anomaly. Finally, we identify the \textit{Liouvillian singular spectrum}, rather than the Liouvillian spectrum itself, as a more fundamental diagnostic of quantum anomalies, and show that it is dual to the operator entanglement spectrum of the mSPT.

</details>


### [131] [Demystifying Relativistic Quantum Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26474)
*R. Muciño,E. Okon,D. Sudarsky,M. Wiedemann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统性地回应了相对论性坍缩理论所面临的主要概念性质疑，并梳理了当前的技术挑战，认为该理论仍为克服标准量子理论概念局限的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论性的客观坍缩理论在解决标准量子力学的概念问题上取得成功，但将其推广到相对论领域的尝试面临诸多概念和技术上的质疑与困难，亟需系统澄清与回应。

Method: 通过哲学与物理相结合的分析方法，逐一检视并反驳关于瞬时坍缩与相对论冲突、属性值与概率的参考系依赖、超光速信号可能性以及叙事性失效等概念性质疑，并对技术性难题进行分类与评估。

Result: 成功驳斥了针对相对论性坍缩理论的主要概念性质疑，澄清了相关误解；同时指出尽管存在如发散问题、协变性构造困难和非标准场需求等技术挑战，但并未构成根本性障碍。

Conclusion: 相对论性坍缩理论仍是构建兼具相对论性与量子性的基础理论框架的一条有前景的路径，值得进一步探索与发展。

Abstract: Non-relativistic objective collapse theories have been remarkably successful in addressing the conceptual problems of standard quantum mechanics. Despite substantial efforts, the project of extending them to the relativistic domain remains burdened by significant conceptual objections and technical challenges, often taken to cast doubt on the viability of the program as a whole. On the conceptual side, relativistic collapse theories have been claimed to face challenges involving tension between instantaneous collapse and relativity, frame-dependence of property values and probabilities, the possibility of superluminal signaling and the failure of narratability. On the technical side, persistent infinities, difficulties in constructing fully covariant frameworks and the apparent need for non-standard fields have hindered the development of workable models. In this paper, we offer a systematic rebuttal of the conceptual objections and provide a structured account of the remaining technical challenges. We conclude that relativistic collapse theories do provide a promising route toward a fully relativistic quantum framework that overcomes the conceptual limitations of standard quantum theory.

</details>


### [132] [Bosonic Encodings for Hermite-Galerkin Discretizations of High-Dimensional PDEs and Bayesian Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26268)
*Alice Barthe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Koopman-von Neumann框架，用于求解具有结构化多项式形式的线性偏微分方程，并证明了在时变动力学和带隙基态问题中截断的收敛性，提出了一种支持高效块编码的量子比特编码方法，并将其应用于高斯先验和观测噪声的贝叶斯反问题中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决非线性动力学中的复杂问题，需要将非线性常微分方程转化为线性偏微分方程，并利用量子算法进行有效处理。

Method: 通过扩展Koopman-von Neumann框架，使用Hermite函数基表示概率幅，并引入新的量子比特编码方式以支持高效的块编码操作。

Result: 证明了在明确的正则性和稳定性假设下，时间依赖动力学和带隙基态问题的截断收敛性；提出了适用于贝叶斯反问题的框架，能够将后验状态制备简化为结构化哈密顿量基态的制备。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架不仅适用于更广泛的线性PDE问题，而且为解决贝叶斯反问题提供了有效的量子算法途径。

Abstract: The Koopman-von Neumann framework has been proposed to design quantum algorithms for non-linear dynamics. It maps a non-linear ordinary differential equation to a linear partial differential equation (PDE) governing a probability amplitude. Previous works represents this amplitude in the Hermite-function basis, equivalently as a bosonic state, and truncates the total Hermite degree to obtain a representation over $Θ(m\log N)$ qubits, where $N$ is the number of variables and $m$ the truncation order. We extend this approach to a broader class of linear PDEs whose differential operators have a structured polynomial form. We prove convergence of the truncation for both time-dependent dynamics and gapped ground-state problems under explicit regularity and stability assumptions. We then introduce a qubit encoding that supports efficient block encodings of the truncated operators. Finally, we apply the framework to Bayesian inverse problems with Gaussian priors and observation noise, reducing posterior-state preparation to the preparation of a structured Hamiltonian's ground state.

</details>


### [133] [Fault-tolerant quantum computation cannot be achieved with constant spacetime overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26272)
*Kishor Bharti,Tobias Haug,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子容错计算中的时空开销下限，发现在量子存储任务中存在不可避免的对数级额外开销，但该开销可在多个逻辑量子比特间分摊，足够宽的计算仍可实现恒定相对开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨量子容错计算中累积时空开销是否可以保持恒定，特别是在量子存储等基本任务中的资源消耗极限。

Method: 在乐观噪声模型下，结合一般自适应协议，分析量子信息存储任务中的累积时空开销，并构造具有正码率的CSS码以达到理论下限，进一步推导子系统时空码的电路规模界限。

Result: 证明了即使在最理想条件下，量子存储任务仍存在不可避免的对数级累积时空开销；提出了可达到该存储下限的正码率CSS码构造方法；发现该开销可在多逻辑量子比特间共享，使得足够大规模的计算（如Shor算法）可能实现恒定相对开销；并给出了子系统时空码的电路规模界限。

Conclusion: 量子容错计算存在基本的资源消耗下限，对数级时空开销无法完全消除，但可通过并行化分摊，为高效容错量子计算的设计提供了理论基础和边界条件。

Abstract: The threshold theorem states that quantum computations can be made reliable below a physical error threshold, at the cost of additional physical qubits and circuit depth. Recent work has reduced these space and time overheads to polylogarithmic or nearly logarithmic scalings, but whether the cumulative spacetime overhead can be constant has remained unclear. Here, we show that even for the simplest task of preserving quantum information in a quantum memory, under an optimistic noise model and allowing general adaptive protocols, there is an unavoidable logarithmic contribution to the cumulative spacetime overhead. This additional cost can nevertheless be shared among many logical qubits, so sufficiently wide computations, including standard implementations of Shor's algorithm, may still achieve constant relative overhead. We further give a positive-rate CSS code construction that attains the memory bound, identify sufficient conditions under which the same scaling extends from quantum memory to fault-tolerant circuit implementations, and derive circuit-size bounds for subsystem spacetime codes. Our work establishes fundamental limits on the resources required for quantum fault tolerance.

</details>


### [134] [Krylov Break Times from an Inhomogeneous Lieb--Robinson Light Cone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27399)
*Shunji Matsuura,Yoji Kawamura,Joseph Salfi,Satoshi Iso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Krylov和Lanczos近似在量子动力学等领域的应用，提出了一种基于Jacobi链上传输度量的新方法来估计有限维Krylov截断的适用时间，并建立了Lieb-Robinson界，表明信息传播受锥形区域限制。误差来源于往返效应，推导出截断动力学误差的下界，并定义了满足精度要求的最大时间（break time）。理论与数值实验一致表明，在传输受限情况下，该时间近似等于累积传输时间τ_m；而在局域激发或谱已解析的情况下，截断可长期有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了确定m维Krylov截断在多长时间内仍能可靠地逼近量子系统的演化，特别是在量子动力学和哈密顿学习中的应用中，需要一个实用的时间尺度判断标准。

Method: 引入非均匀传输度量ρ(m,n)，基于Lanczos过程中得到的Jacobi链上的跃迁强度b_j，结合因果传播思想建立Lieb-Robinson不等式，并利用Duhamel公式分析截断引起的动力学误差。通过理论推导得出break time的下界，并辅以自旋链和随机Jacobi矩阵的数值验证。

Result: 证明了在传输度量ρ下的Lieb-Robinson速度为v_LR=2，信息传播被限制在ρ(m,n) ≃ 2|t|的锥内；返回振幅的误差来自信息往返边界的过程；得出break time t_*(m;ε) ≥ τ_m[1-o(1)]，且在传播情形下近似取等t_* ≃ τ_m；当激发局域或谱已被分辨时，误差极小，截断可长期有效；数值结果支持理论预测。

Conclusion: Krylov截断的有效时间由Jacobi链上的因果传播决定，其核心是基于Lanczos系数的非均匀度量ρ(m,n)；在广泛情形下，break time近似为τ_m = ∑_{j<m} 1/b_j；但在某些特殊初态下，截断可能始终精确，此时有效维度m_*后不再有时间限制。

Abstract: Krylov and Lanczos approximations are used in quantum dynamics, quantum subspace methods, and Hamiltonian learning. A practical question is how long an $m$-dimensional Krylov truncation can be trusted. We argue that this time is fixed by causal propagation on the associated Jacobi chain. The relevant distance is not the Krylov index itself, but the inhomogeneous transport metric $ρ(m,n) = \sum_{j=\min(m,n)}^{\max(m,n)-1} 1/b_j$, where $b_j$ is the Lanczos hopping across the bond $j \leftrightarrow j+1$. We prove a Lieb--Robinson bound in this metric. Its small-weight limit gives the velocity $v_{\rm LR} = 2$, meaning that propagation is exponentially suppressed outside the cone $ρ(m,n) \simeq 2|t|$. The error of a finite Krylov approximation to the return amplitude is a round-trip effect: information has to travel from the probe to the truncation boundary and back. Combining the Lieb--Robinson bound with Duhamel's formula yields a lower bound on the error of the truncated dynamics. For a fixed tolerance $ε$, let the break time $t_\ast(m;ε)$ denote the longest time for which the $m$-dimensional truncation is guaranteed to reproduce the exact return amplitude within error $ε$. We show that $t_\ast(m;ε) \ge τ_m[1-o(1)]$, where $τ_m = ρ(0,m) = \sum_{j<m} 1/b_j$. When the probe spreads along the chain, this lower bound is also tight, so $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$. The situation is different when the probe excites only a localized part of the spectrum, or a part already resolved by the truncation. In this case, essentially no signal reaches the boundary. Beyond a state-dependent Krylov dimension $m_\ast$, the approximation can therefore remain accurate at all times, and the break time is effectively infinite. Numerical tests on spin chains and random Jacobi matrices support $t_\ast(m) \simeq τ_m$ in the transport-limited regime.

</details>


### [135] [Quantizing the guiding center: do quantization and coarse graining commute?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26277)
*Rodrigo Andrade e Silva,Ted Jacobson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种非微扰的、基于群论的引导中心理论量子化方法，用于描述二维磁场中带电粒子的漂移运动，并比较了该有效理论与底层微观理论在量子化后的对应关系。结果表明，尽管通常情况下两者预测一致，但在闭合等磁轮廓下，有效理论会错误地预言一种空间离散性（“半径”量子化），而这种现象在微观理论中并不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解在对有效理论进行量子化时，是否可能产生与微观理论不一致的非微扰虚假预言，特别是在引导中心近似下丢失小尺度回旋运动信息的情况下。

Method: 采用非微扰和群论方法对引导中心有效理论进行量子化，将物理空间作为约化相空间，导致坐标非对易；并通过制定微观与有效量子理论之间的匹配方案进行对比分析。

Result: 发现有效理论量子化后通常与微观理论一致，但在闭合等磁线情况下，有效理论预言了不存在于微观理论中的‘半径’量子化，即出现虚假的空间离散性。

Conclusion: 有效理论的量子化可能产生看似合理但实际为虚假的非微扰预言，即使该量子理论自身没有明显失效迹象，也需谨慎对待其物理结论。

Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative, group-theoretic quantization of the effective guiding center theory of a charged particle drifting in a magnetic field in two spatial dimensions, and compare the resulting quantum theory with a corresponding coarse graining of the underlying microscopic theory. In the classical effective theory, the small gyro motion is not resolved, while the motion of the center of the gyro orbit remains observable. The reduced phase space is the physical space itself, so quantization leads to noncommuting spatial coordinates, and the effective theory loses access to the metric structure of physical space, retaining only its area structure. By formulating a prescription to match between the microscopic and effective quantum theories, we find that the predictions of the quantized effective theory are generally consistent with those of the microscopic theory. However, for closed isomagnetic contours the effective theory predicts a quantization of ``radius'', a spatial discreteness absent from the microscopic theory. This illustrates that quantization of an effective theory may yield spurious nonperturbative predictions, even if that quantum theory shows no internal signs of breakdown.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamics of local quantum information in random unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27440)
*Ratul Thakur,Sthitadhi Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种解析局域量子信息流动的细粒度理论，通过信息格框架揭示了信息尺度分布的动力学，并在随机酉电路和Clifford电路中发现具有普适性的Tracy-Widom分布与t^{1/3}涨落标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对量子多体系统中信息 scrambling 的描述（如纠缠或算符增长）过于粗粒化，缺乏对信息在不同空间区域和尺度间流动的微观机制的理解，因此需要一种能分辨长度尺度的信息动力学理论。

Method: 引入“信息格”框架以系统化描述各子系统和各长度尺度上的信息内容，结合局域随机酉电路模型，将信息尺度分布的动力学映射为精确的经典随机过程；对Clifford电路则构建稳定子生成元演化的马尔可夫模型进行分析。

Result: 发现信息尺度分布在时间上以 ballistic 方式传播（∝ t），并伴随 t^{1/3} 的展宽，其形式符合Tracy-Widom分布；该行为在Haar随机电路和Clifford电路中均存在，表明结果具有普适性。

Conclusion: 建立了局域量子信息在多尺度下演化的普适动力学图像，揭示了信息从微观到宏观流动的统一规律，为连接微观信息动力学与宏观流体力学描述提供了可能路径。

Abstract: The physics of information scrambling in quantum many-body systems is intimately related to thermalisation and emergence of chaos. However, its standard characterisation through bipartite entanglement or operator growth remains inherently coarse-grained, obscuring the spatiotemporal anatomy of how quantum information flows between different regions of the system and across various length scales. In this work, we develop a theory for the dynamics of local information in local random unitary circuits which resolves the fine-grained microscopic structures of local information flow in such systems. Using the framework of information lattice which systematises the information content at each length scale within each subsystem, we identify a distribution of length scales at which information resides and obtain the dynamics of the distribution. For Haar-random circuits with infinite local Hilbert-space dimensions, we map this dynamics onto an exact classical stochastic process which reveals that this distribution is described by a Tracy-Widom form which moves ballistically in time ($ \propto t$) toward larger scales, accompanied by a $\sim t^{1/3}$ broadening. We also find the same qualitative behaviour for random Clifford circuits acting on qubits. The similar scaling behaviour in two rather different settings hints strongly towards the universality of our results. In the case of Clifford circuits, we develop a phenomenological Markov process for the dynamics of the stabiliser generators in a specific gauge which confirms the Tracy-Widom distribution and the $t^{1/3}$ scaling of the fluctuations. Ultimately, our results establish the universal properties of the dynamics of local quantum information in a length scale-resolved fashion and provide a possible route towards bridging exact microscopic theories for information dynamics with emergent hydrodynamic descriptions of information flow.

</details>


### [137] [Exploiting overcompleteness of Platonic-solid POVMs for shadow estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26318)
*Dario Melegari,Stefano Mangini,Keijo Korhonen,Hetta Vappula,Daniel Cavalcanti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于柏拉图固体POVM的量子测量方法，通过优化对偶框架和测量方向，提升分子哈密顿量的估计精度，并揭示经典预处理与估计准确性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确从有限测量中估计可观测量的期望值是量子信息科学的核心挑战，现有信息过完备测量虽能降低估计方差，但其测量几何与对偶框架构建的关联尚不明确。

Method: 采用对应于布洛赫球上五种柏拉图立体顶点的高度对称单量子比特测量（Platonic solid POVMs），结合k-局部最优对偶框架，并提出通过经典代理态（如乘积态或矩阵乘积态）联合优化POVM方向与效应权重。

Result: 发现POVM的几何结构对估计方差具有非平凡且非单调的影响；优化后的柏拉图固体POVM在足够表征目标态的MPS键维下可超越标准随机Pauli测量。

Conclusion: 测量几何的优化可在经典预处理与估计精度之间实现有利权衡，为近期量子设备上的可观测量估计提供了实用改进路径。

Abstract: Accurately estimating expectation values of observables from a finite number of measurement shots is a central challenge in quantum information science. Informationally overcomplete measurements provide a route to reduce estimation variance through optimized classical post-processing. However, the interplay between measurement geometry and dual-frame construction remains largely unexplored. In this work, we study Platonic solid POVMs ---highly symmetric, overcomplete single-qubit measurements whose effects correspond to the vertices of the five Platonic solids on the Bloch sphere--- for the estimation of molecular Hamiltonians. Using $k$-locally optimal dual frames, we show that the geometry of the POVM can have a non-trivial and non-monotonic effect on the estimation variance. We further propose a joint optimization of the POVM orientation and effect weights using a classical proxy state, either a product state or a Matrix Product State (MPS) approximation. We demonstrate that optimized Platonic solid POVMs can outperform standard randomized Pauli measurements, provided the MPS bond dimension is sufficient to faithfully represent the target state. These results reveal a trade-off between classical preprocessing and estimation accuracy, suggesting a practical route to improved observable estimation on near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [138] [Enhancing the phase sensitivity of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer beyond the Heisenberg limit through dynamic squeezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26350)
*Lakshya Bhardwaj,Ankit Tiwari,Mohan Sarovar,Christian Arenz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过在干涉仪一臂应用正交分量的动态压缩序列来增强Mach-Zehnder干涉仪相位灵敏度的方法，该方法不依赖输入态，并可实现超海森堡极限的灵敏度，且对适度光子损耗具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在不依赖输入态的情况下提升Mach-Zehnder干涉仪的相位测量灵敏度，探索超越标准量子极限的通用方案。

Method: 在干涉仪的一臂中引入交替的正交分量压缩操作，形成动态压缩序列，并结合非经典输入态进行灵敏度优化。

Result: 实现了与输入态无关的相位灵敏度增强，达到了超海森堡极限的标度，并验证了方案在存在适度光子损耗时的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的动态压缩方法为量子传感提供了一种普适且实用的灵敏度增强途径，可与现有非经典态方案兼容并进一步提升性能。

Abstract: We propose a method to enhance the phase sensitivity of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer, independent of its input states. This is achieved by applying squeezing sequences to one arm of the interferometer. By alternating between squeezing along orthogonal quadratures, we demonstrate that the phase sensitivity of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer can be generically enhanced. Since this enhancement is independent of the input states, the proposed dynamic squeezing method allows for further improvement in phase sensitivity when combined with non-classical input states. We compare the dynamic squeezing approach with existing quantum sensing protocols and show that super-Heisenberg scaling can be achieved. Finally, we demonstrate that the scheme is robust against moderate photon loss.

</details>


### [139] [Misunderstanding Convivial Solipsism: the Necessity of a Perspectival Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26361)
*Herve Zwirn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了“共悦唯我论”在面对如维格纳朋友等思想实验时常见的误解，强调观察事实必须与特定视角相关联，避免将其视为绝对的全局事实，从而防止出现看似矛盾的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清对共悦唯我论的常见误解，尤其是在处理非绝对事实和量子力学解释时的概念混淆。

Method: 通过分析维格纳朋友及其扩展场景中的反对意见，揭示这些误解源于将被该理论否定的经典假设重新引入讨论中，并提出应以视角化语言表述观察事实。

Result: 表明观察事实需索引到具体视角，例如‘从某人视角看’，否则会导致虚假的两难困境；同时论证共悦唯我论并非心理上的唯我论，而是一种视角化言说的规范。

Conclusion: 共悦唯我论要求严格的视角化陈述，其逻辑结构类似于量子概率相对于经典概率的地位，有助于正确理解意识、交流与主体间性问题。

Abstract: Convivial Solipsism is easily misunderstood. Because it denies the absoluteness of observed events while preserving the universality of quantum mechanics, it invites objections that are often formulated in a classical, non-perspectival language. Such objections appear powerful only because they smuggle back into the discussion the very assumptions that Convivial Solipsism rejects. This article reconstructs a family of misunderstandings that arise in discussions of Convivial Solipsism, especially in connection with Wigner's friend and extended Wigner's friend scenarios. The aim is not merely defensive. The objections are useful because they identify the conceptual discipline required by any interpretation that takes non-absolute facts seriously. The central claim is that observational facts must be indexed to perspectives. A statement such as "the friend saw outcome A" is not a complete factual statement within Convivial Solipsism. It must be reformulated as "from the friend's perspective, the friend saw outcome A" or "from Wigner's perspective, the friend reported outcome B." The mistake consists in removing these indices and then treating the resulting expressions as if they belonged to a single global factual domain. This leads to apparent contradictions and to false dilemmas concerning consciousness, communication, other minds, and intersubjectivity. When that is taken into account, it appears that Convivial Solipsism is not a retreat into psychological solipsism, but a discipline of perspectival discourse. It is also shown that perspectival logic stands to classical logic as the quantum calculus of probability stands to Kolmogorovian probability theory.

</details>


### [140] [Recovery-Free CHSH Nonlocality with Particle Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26407)
*Giovanni Scala,Cosmo Lupo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了在无显式恢复操作的情况下，CHSH非局域性是否能在粒子丢失中存活，并提出了基于不同擦除模型和编码方案的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在不进行显式恢复操作的前提下，CHSH非局域性在粒子丢失环境下的鲁棒性，区分由损耗本身与测量方式带来的根本限制。

Method: 通过区分标记性擦除和非标记性删除两种模型，结合已知的量子容量结果设计渐近逼近量子极限的测量方案；构造基于n-粒子Dicke态的置换不变编码及针对幸存粒子的测量，提出无需恢复的显式协议。

Result: 对于标记性擦除且存活概率η>1/2时可实现CHSH违背，η≤1/2时两种模型下均不可能；单激发编码可在η>1/√2时违背CHSH，增加激发数N可使阈值提升至黄金比例(√5−1)/2≈0.618；还构造了能容忍O(√n)删除错误的稀疏删除二项式协议。

Conclusion: CHSH非局域性可在特定条件下无需恢复操作而抵抗粒子丢失，其存活性取决于损耗类型、存活概率以及所采用的编码与测量策略。

Abstract: Can CHSH nonlocality survive particle loss without applying an explicit recovery operation? In principle, any deterministic recovery can be absorbed into the measurement. Operationally, we show that the answer depends on the allowed measurements on the lossy system. We distinguish flagged erasure, in which each lost particle leaves a detectable record, from unflagged deletion, in which no such record remains. For flagged erasure and survival probability $η>1/2$, using known quantum-capacity results, we show measurements that asymptotically approach the quantum maximum $2\sqrt2$. In contrast, for $η\le1/2$, CHSH violation is impossible for both loss models. Then, we construct explicit recovery-free protocols using permutation-invariant encodings built from $n$-qubit Dicke states $| D_N^n\rangle$ and measurements on the surviving particles. A one-excitation $(N=1)$ encoding violates CHSH for $η>1/\sqrt{2}$. Increasing the excitation number $N$ yields a family of protocols that violates CHSH for $η>η_G=(\sqrt{5}-1)/2$, with the asymptotic golden ratio approached as $N \to \infty$. Finally, we present a sparse-deletion binomial PI protocol that guarantees CHSH violation for up to $O(\sqrt n)$ deletion errors. Our results distinguish fundamental limits imposed by loss from those set by explicit measurements without recovery.

</details>


### [141] [The Role of Geometric Analysis in Interferometer Design and Optimization for Gravitational Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26420)
*Alireza Maleki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过两个大质量粒子之间的量子纠缠来探测引力的量子性质的实验方案，特别分析了干涉仪几何结构中常被忽略的垂直段相位贡献。结果表明，忽略这些部分会导致错误甚至零结果，而精确考虑全轨迹对预测和观察引力诱导纠缠至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于普朗克尺度下的直接实验极难实现，寻找能够验证量子引力效应的可行实验方案是当前重要课题。近期提出的利用引力诱导纠缠的实验设计提供了新路径，但其中常用近似可能影响结论准确性，因此需要更精确分析。

Method: 通过对干涉仪中粒子完整轨迹进行建模，推导包含垂直段相位贡献的精确解，并与忽略垂直段的近似方法对比，分析不同参数下诱导纠缠的变化情况。

Result: 发现忽略垂直段相位会显著影响相位累积，在某些情况下导致本应出现的纠缠消失；识别出纠缠完全消失的特定构型，这是简化模型未能捕捉到的关键现象。

Conclusion: 完整干涉仪几何结构的影响不可忽视，精确处理粒子轨迹对于正确评估引力诱导纠缠至关重要，这对未来相关实验设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: The pursuit of a quantum theory of gravity, aiming to unify general relativity and quantum mechanics, remains one of the most enduring challenges in physics. Because of the extreme energy scales associated with the Planck regime, direct experimental evidence for quantum gravity remains elusive. However, recent proposals suggest that quantum entanglement between two massive particles may provide a pathway to probe the quantum nature of gravity. In this study, we examine the interferometer geometries proposed in these works, with particular attention to the commonly used approximation that neglects phase contributions from the vertical segments of the particle trajectories. Our analysis shows that this approximation can lead to incorrect predictions and, in certain parameter regimes, to null results where entanglement would otherwise be expected. We derive exact solutions that incorporate the full particle trajectories and demonstrate that the vertical arms can significantly affect the accumulated phase. Crucially, we identify configurations in which the induced entanglement vanishes entirely, a feature missed by simplified treatments. These findings show that accounting for the full interferometer geometry is not merely a refinement, but is essential for accurately assessing gravity-induced entanglement.

</details>


### [142] [Emergent non-Markovianity in time-delayed waveguide QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26427)
*Debsuvra Mukhopadhyay,Stuart J. Masson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究表明，在具有单激发的时延波导量子电动力学系统中，即使延迟时间很短，集体效应也会导致非马尔可夫性，且记忆效应具有内在的状态依赖性，提示在多体量子系统中应用马尔可夫近似需谨慎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在量子多体系统中环境记忆效应的影响，特别是集体效应如何导致非马尔可夫动力学，以评估马尔可夫近似的适用性。

Method: 采用时延波导QED模型，固定最大传播时间并增加发射体数量，分析超辐射和亚辐射态下的系统与环境特征时间尺度。

Result: 发现即使在小延迟下，集体效应也能引发显著的非马可夫性；超辐射态中系统时间尺度为超辐射寿命，环境时间尺度为端到端延迟；亚辐射态中两者均依赖于具体态结构。

Conclusion: 在扩展量子器件至大规模系统时，必须谨慎对待马尔可夫近似，因其可能忽略关键的集体记忆效应。

Abstract: Quantum systems are invariably coupled to a surrounding environment. A common theoretical method to simplify the problem is to trace over the environment under a Markov approximation, which assumes that the environment holds no memory over the timescales that the system evolves under. In interacting many-body systems, it is often not trivial to extract which of the multiple collective timescales are relevant. We consider a time-delayed waveguide QED setup with a single excitation. By fixing the maximum propagation time and increasing the number of emitters, we show that, even for small delay times, collective effects are sufficient to cause non-Markovianity. The impact of memory is intrinsically state-dependent.
